background_color = '#fef9ef'

# create figure and axis

fig.set_facecolor(background_color)
ax.set_facecolor(background_color)

def update(frame):
    ax.clear()
    subset_df = pivot_df.iloc[:frame]
    ax.stackplot(
        subset_df.index,
        subset_df.values.T,
        colors=colors
    )

    # description of video
    text = """
    Evolution of the number of object put in outer
    space each year by the <USA> and the <Russia>
    """
    fig_text(
        0.127, 0.84,
        text,
        ha='left', va='top',
        fontsize=20,
        color='black',
        highlight_textprops=[
                {'color':colors[1],
                'fontweight':'bold'},
                {'color':colors[0],
                'fontweight':'bold'}
        ],
        fig=fig
    )

    # credit annotation
    text = """
    Developed by <@joseph_barbier>
    Data Source: <Office for Outer Space Affairs>
    """
    fig_text(
        0.9, 0,
        text,
        ha='right', va='bottom',
        fontsize=7,
        color='black',
        highlight_textprops=[
            {'fontweight':'bold'},
            {'fontweight':'bold'},
        ],
        fig=fig
    )

    return ax

ani = FuncAnimation(
    fig=fig,
    func=update,
    frames=len(pivot_df),
    interval=10 # in ms
)

dpi = 30
plt.rcParams['figure.dpi'] = dpi

# parameters
colors = ['#2a9d8f', '#e76f51']
background_color = '#fef9ef'

# create figure and axis
fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(12, 8))
fig.set_facecolor(background_color)
ax.set_facecolor(background_color)

def update(frame):
    ax.clear()
    subset_df = pivot_df.iloc[:frame]
    ax.stackplot(
        subset_df.index,
        subset_df.values.T,
        colors=colors
    )

    # custom axis
    factor_y_axis = 1.9
    y_max = pivot_df.iloc[:frame+1].sum(axis=1).max()*factor_y_axis
    ax.set_ylim(0, y_max)
    ax.set_xticks([])
    ax.spines[['top', 'right', 'bottom']].set_visible(False)

    # description of video
    text = """
    Evolution of the number of object put in outer
    space each year by the <USA> and the <Russia>
    """
    fig_text(
        0.127, 0.84,
        text,
        ha='left', va='top',
        fontsize=20,
        color='black',
        highlight_textprops=[
                {'color':colors[1],
                'fontweight':'bold'},
                {'color':colors[0],
                'fontweight':'bold'}
        ],
        fig=fig
    )

    # credit annotation
    text = """
    Developed by <@joseph_barbier>
    Data Source: <Office for Outer Space Affairs>
    """
    fig_text(
        0.9, 0,
        text,
        ha='right', va='bottom',
        fontsize=7,
        color='black',
        highlight_textprops=[
            {'fontweight':'bold'},
            {'fontweight':'bold'},
        ],
        fig=fig
    )

    return ax

ani = FuncAnimation(
    fig=fig,
    func=update,
    frames=len(pivot_df),
    interval=10 # in ms
)
ani.save('../../static/animations/web-animation-with-text3.gif', writer='imagemagick', fps=5)31:Tc9b,# control resolution (lower is faster)
dpi = 30
plt.rcParams['figure.dpi'] = dpi
plt.rcParams['savefig.dpi'] = dpi

# parameters
colors = ['#2a9d8f', '#e76f51']
background_color = '#fef9ef'

# reference values
total = pivot_df_reference.sum().sum()
us_share = pivot_df_reference.loc[:,'USA'].sum()/total*100
rus_share = 100 - us_share
data_after_2012 = pivot_df_reference.loc[pivot_df_reference.index >= 2012]
us_share_after_2012 = data_after_2012['USA'].sum()/data_after_2012.sum().sum()*100

# important dates
start_date = 1958
end_date = 2023

# description
description = f"""Between {start_date} and {end_date}, the USA and Russia have launched in outer space a total of {int(total)} objects. The USA has launched {us_share:.2f}% of these objects, while the Russia has only launched {rus_share:.2f}% of them.\n

After 2012, {us_share_after_2012:.2f}% of the objects launched in outer space by the USA and Russia were from the USA, highlighting the increasing dominance of the USA in outer space exploration.
"""

# create figure and axis
fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(12, 8))
fig.set_facecolor(background_color)
ax.set_facecolor(background_color)

def update(frame):
    ax.clear()
    subset_df = pivot_df.iloc[:frame]
    ax.stackplot(
        subset_df.index,
        subset_df.values.T,
        colors=colors
    )

    # custom axis
    factor_y_axis = 1.9
    y_max = pivot_df.iloc[:frame+1].sum(axis=1).max()*factor_y_axis
    ax.set_ylim(0, y_max)
    ax.set_xticks([])
    ax.spines[['top', 'right', 'bottom']].set_visible(False)

    # description of video
    text = """
    Evolution of the number of object put in outer
    space each year by the <USA> and the <Russia>
    """
    fig_text(
        0.127, 0.84,
        text,
        ha='left', va='top',
        fontsize=20,
        color='black',
        highlight_textprops=[
                {'color':colors[1],
                'fontweight':'bold'},
                {'color':colors[0],
                'fontweight':'bold'}
        ],
        fig=fig
    )

    # credit annotation
    text = """
    Developed by <@joseph_barbier>
    Data Source: <Office for Outer Space Affairs>
    """
    fig_text(
        0.9, 0,
        text,
        ha='right', va='bottom',
        fontsize=7,
        color='black',
        highlight_textprops=[
            {'fontweight':'bold'},
            {'fontweight':'bold'},
        ],
        fig=fig
    )

    # get text to display at current frame
    total_chars = len(description)
    effective_frame_count = len(pivot_df) - 20 # ends 20 frames before the end
    num_chars = math.ceil(total_chars * (frame / effective_frame_count)) if frame < effective_frame_count else total_chars
    current_description = description[:num_chars]
    wrapped_text = '\n'.join([textwrap.fill(paragraph, width=70) for paragraph in current_description.split('\n')])

    fig_text(
        0.157, 0.72,
        wrapped_text,
        ha='left', va='top',
        fontsize=14,
        color='black',
        fig=fig
    )

    return ax

ani = FuncAnimation(
    fig=fig,
    func=update,
    frames=len(pivot_df),
    interval=10 # in ms
)
ani.save('../../static/animations/web-animation-with-text4.gif', writer='imagemagick', fps=5)32:T1622,# control resolution (lower is faster)
dpi = 30
plt.rcParams['figure.dpi'] = dpi

# parameters
colors = ['#2a9d8f', '#e76f51']
background_color = '#fef9ef'

# reference values
total = pivot_df_reference.sum().sum()
us_share = pivot_df_reference.loc[:,'USA'].sum()/total*100
rus_share = 100 - us_share
data_after_2012 = pivot_df_reference.loc[pivot_df_reference.index >= 2012]
us_share_after_2012 = data_after_2012['USA'].sum()/data_after_2012.sum().sum()*100

# important dates
start_date = 1958
end_date = 2023
first_human_spaceflight = 1961
us_moon_landing = 1969
hubble_launch = 1990
internat_space_station = 1998
earth_size_found = 2017
photo_black_hole = 2019
total_objects_today = 2023

# delay for each event
delay_years = 1 # to display the event one year after it happened
dates_highlight = [
    first_human_spaceflight + delay_years,
    us_moon_landing + delay_years,
    hubble_launch + delay_years,
    internat_space_station + delay_years,
    earth_size_found + delay_years,
    photo_black_hole + delay_years,
]
event_end_dates = dates_highlight[1:] + [end_date + delay_years]

# position adjustments for each event
adjustements = [
    (0, 0),
    (0, 10),
    (2, 5),
    (0, 20),
    (-18, 0),
    (-22, -400),
]

# text to display for each event
event_texts = [
    f'First Human Spaceflight\n<{first_human_spaceflight}>',
    f'US Moon Landing\n<{us_moon_landing}>',
    f'Hubble Launch\n<{hubble_launch}>',
    f'International Space Station\n<{internat_space_station}>',
    f'Earth Size Planets found\n<{earth_size_found}>',
    f'First Image of a Black Hole\n<{photo_black_hole}>'
]

# description
description = f"""Between {start_date} and {end_date}, the USA and Russia have launched in outer space a total of {int(total)} objects. The USA has launched {us_share:.2f}% of these objects, while the Russia has only launched {rus_share:.2f}% of them.\n

After 2012, {us_share_after_2012:.2f}% of the objects launched in outer space by the USA and Russia were from the USA, highlighting the increasing dominance of the USA in outer space exploration.
"""

# create figure and axis
fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(12, 8))
fig.set_facecolor(background_color)
ax.set_facecolor(background_color)

# functions use to annotate events with black lollipops
def annot_event(current_date, event_start_date, event_end_date, adj, text):

   # condition to only display one event at a time
   if event_start_date <= current_date <= event_end_date:

      # filter on the current year
      sub_subset = pivot_df.loc[event_start_date]
      total_objects_thisyear = sub_subset['RUS'] + sub_subset['USA']

      # plot lollipop
      ax.plot((event_start_date, event_start_date), (0, total_objects_thisyear), color='black', zorder=10)
      ax.scatter(event_start_date, total_objects_thisyear, color='black', s=100, zorder=10)

      # annotate event
      ax_text(
         event_start_date - 2 + adj[0], # x position
         total_objects_thisyear*1.2 + adj[1], # y position
         text,
         fontsize=14,
         color='black',
         ha='left', va='center',
         highlight_textprops=[
               {'fontweight':'bold'}
         ],
         ax=ax
      )

def update(frame):
   ax.clear()
   subset_df = pivot_df.iloc[:frame]
   ax.stackplot(
      subset_df.index,
      subset_df.values.T,
      colors=colors
   )

   # custom axis
   factor_y_axis = 1.9
   y_max = pivot_df.iloc[:frame+1].sum(axis=1).max()*factor_y_axis
   ax.set_ylim(0, y_max)
   ax.set_xticks([])
   ax.spines[['top', 'right', 'bottom']].set_visible(False)

   # year annotation in background
   year = pivot_df.index[frame]
   fig_text(
      0.15, 0.9,
      '1958 - ' + str(round(year)),
      ha='left', va='center',
      fontsize=50,
      fontweight='bold',
      alpha=0.1,
      fig=fig
   )

   # description of video
   text = """
   Evolution of the number of object put in outer
   space each year by the <USA> and the <Russia>
   """
   fig_text(
      0.127, 0.84,
      text,
      ha='left', va='top',
      fontsize=20,
      color='black',
      highlight_textprops=[
               {'color':colors[1],
               'fontweight':'bold'},
               {'color':colors[0],
               'fontweight':'bold'}
      ],
      fig=fig
   )

   # credit annotation
   text = """
   Developed by <@joseph_barbier>
   Data Source: <Office for Outer Space Affairs>
   """
   fig_text(
      0.9, 0,
      text,
      ha='right', va='bottom',
      fontsize=7,
      color='black',
      highlight_textprops=[
         {'fontweight':'bold'},
         {'fontweight':'bold'},
      ],
      fig=fig
   )

   # get text to display at current frame
   total_chars = len(description)
   effective_frame_count = len(pivot_df) - 20 # ends 20 frames before the end
   num_chars = math.ceil(total_chars * (frame / effective_frame_count)) if frame < effective_frame_count else total_chars
   current_description = description[:num_chars]
   wrapped_text = '\n'.join([textwrap.fill(paragraph, width=70) for paragraph in current_description.split('\n')])

   # display description
   fig_text(
      0.157, 0.72,
      wrapped_text,
      ha='left', va='top',
      fontsize=14,
      color='black',
      fig=fig
   )

   # annotate events
   for event_date, end_date, adj, event_text in zip(dates_highlight, event_end_dates, adjustements, event_texts):
      annot_event(year, event_date, end_date, adj, event_text)

   return ax

# get initial frames
with Image.open(path) as img:
    frames = [frame.copy() for frame in ImageSequence.Iterator(img)]

# set duration of each frame
initial_duration = 50 # in ms
for i, frame in enumerate(frames):

   # default duration
   frame_duration = initial_duration

   # make last N frames becomes step by step slower
   N = 12
   if i > len(frames) - N:
      frame_duration = initial_duration + (i - len(frames) + N) * 50

   # stay for last date
   if i == len(frames) - 1:
      frame_duration = 10000

   # assign duration to the frame
   frame.info['duration'] = frame_duration

# save the frames
frames[0].save(path, save_all=True, append_images=frames[1:], loop=0)34:T4dc,# interpolate for more data points
multiple = 5
new_index = np.linspace(pivot_df.index.min(), pivot_df.index.max(), len(pivot_df) * multiple-(multiple-1))
pivot_df = pivot_df.reindex(new_index).interpolate(method='linear')
pivot_df.reset_index(inplace=True)
pivot_df.rename(columns={'index': 'Year'}, inplace=True)
pivot_df['Year'] = pivot_df['Year'].astype(int)
pivot_df.index = pivot_df['Year']
pivot_df.drop(columns='Year', inplace=True)
pivot_df.index = new_index

# higher interpolate level after 2018
pivot_df_before = pivot_df.loc[:2018]
pivot_df_after = pivot_df.loc[2018:]
multiple_end = 15
new_index_end = np.linspace(pivot_df_after.index.min(), pivot_df_after.index.max(), len(pivot_df_after) * multiple_end-(multiple_end-1))
pivot_df_after = pivot_df_after.reindex(new_index_end).interpolate(method='linear')
pivot_df_after.reset_index(inplace=True)
pivot_df_after.rename(columns={'index': 'Year'}, inplace=True)
pivot_df_after['Year'] = pivot_df_after['Year'].astype(int)
pivot_df_after.index = pivot_df_after['Year']
pivot_df_after.drop(columns='Year', inplace=True)
pivot_df_after.index = new_index_end

# combine data
pivot_df = pd.concat([pivot_df_before, pivot_df_after])
pivot_df.drop_duplicates(inplace=True)

   else:

      # annotate events
      for event_date, end_date, adj, event_text in zip(dates_highlight, event_end_dates, adjustements, event_texts):
         annot_event(year, event_date, end_date, adj, event_text)

   return ax

    fig=fig,
    func=update,
    frames=len(pivot_df),
    interval=10 # in ms
)
path = '../../static/animations/web-animation-with-text7.gif'
ani.save(path, writer='imagemagick', fps=5)

# get initial frames
with Image.open(path) as img:
    frames = [frame.copy() for frame in ImageSequence.Iterator(img)]

# set duration of each frame
initial_duration = 50 # in ms
for i, frame in enumerate(frames):

   # default duration
   frame_duration = initial_duration

   # make last N frames becomes step by step slower
   N = 12
   if i > len(frames) - N:
      frame_duration = initial_duration + (i - len(frames) + N) * 50

   # stay for last date
   if i == len(frames) - 1:
      frame_duration = 10000

   # assign duration to the frame
   frame.info['duration'] = frame_duration

# save the frames
frames[0].save(path, save_all=True, append_images=frames[1:], loop=0)36:T1cbb,# control resolution (lower is faster)
dpi = 120
plt.rcParams['figure.dpi'] = dpi
plt.rcParams['savefig.dpi'] = dpi

# parameters
colors = ['#2a9d8f', '#e76f51']
background_color = '#fef9ef'

# reference values
total = pivot_df_reference.sum().sum()
us_share = pivot_df_reference.loc[:,'USA'].sum()/total*100
rus_share = 100 - us_share
data_after_2012 = pivot_df_reference.loc[pivot_df_reference.index >= 2012]
us_share_after_2012 = data_after_2012['USA'].sum()/data_after_2012.sum().sum()*100

# important dates
start_date = 1958
end_date = 2023
first_human_spaceflight = 1961
us_moon_landing = 1969
hubble_launch = 1990
internat_space_station = 1998
earth_size_found = 2017
photo_black_hole = 2019
total_objects_today = 2023

# delay for each event
delay_years = 1 # to display the event one year after it happened
dates_highlight = [
    first_human_spaceflight + delay_years,
    us_moon_landing + delay_years,
    hubble_launch + delay_years,
    internat_space_station + delay_years,
    earth_size_found + delay_years,
    photo_black_hole + delay_years,
]
event_end_dates = dates_highlight[1:] + [end_date + delay_years]

# position adjustments for each event
adjustements = [
    (0, 0),
    (0, 10),
    (2, 5),
    (0, 20),
    (-18, 0),
    (-22, -400),
]

# text to display for each event
event_texts = [
    f'First Human Spaceflight\n<{first_human_spaceflight}>',
    f'US Moon Landing\n<{us_moon_landing}>',
    f'Hubble Launch\n<{hubble_launch}>',
    f'International Space Station\n<{internat_space_station}>',
    f'Earth Size Planets found\n<{earth_size_found}>',
    f'First Image of a Black Hole\n<{photo_black_hole}>'
]

# description
description = f"""Between {start_date} and {end_date}, the USA and Russia have launched in outer space a total of {int(total)} objects. The USA has launched {us_share:.2f}% of these objects, while the Russia has only launched {rus_share:.2f}% of them.\n

After 2012, {us_share_after_2012:.2f}% of the objects launched in outer space by the USA and Russia were from the USA, highlighting the increasing dominance of the USA in outer space exploration.
"""

# create figure and axis
fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(12, 8))
fig.set_facecolor(background_color)
ax.set_facecolor(background_color)

# functions use to annotate events with black lollipops
def annot_event(current_date, event_start_date, event_end_date, adj, text):

   # condition to only display one event at a time
   if event_start_date <= current_date <= event_end_date:

      # filter on the current year
      sub_subset = pivot_df.loc[event_start_date]
      total_objects_thisyear = sub_subset['RUS'] + sub_subset['USA']

      # plot lollipop
      ax.plot((event_start_date, event_start_date), (0, total_objects_thisyear), color='black', zorder=10)
      ax.scatter(event_start_date, total_objects_thisyear, color='black', s=100, zorder=10)

      # annotate event
      ax_text(
         event_start_date - 2 + adj[0], # x position
         total_objects_thisyear*1.2 + adj[1], # y position
         text,
         fontsize=14,
         color='black',
         ha='left', va='center',
         highlight_textprops=[
               {'fontweight':'bold'}
         ],
         ax=ax
      )

def update(frame):
   ax.clear()
   subset_df = pivot_df.iloc[:frame]
   ax.stackplot(
      subset_df.index,
      subset_df.values.T,
      colors=colors
   )

   # custom axis
   factor_y_axis = 1.9
   y_max = pivot_df.iloc[:frame+1].sum(axis=1).max()*factor_y_axis
   ax.set_ylim(0, y_max)
   ax.set_xticks([])
   ax.spines[['top', 'right', 'bottom']].set_visible(False)

   # year annotation in background
   year = pivot_df.index[frame]
   fig_text(
      0.15, 0.9,
      '1958 - ' + str(round(year)),
      ha='left', va='center',
      fontsize=50,
      fontweight='bold',
      alpha=0.1,
      fig=fig
   )

   # description of video
   text = """
   Evolution of the number of object put in outer
   space each year by the <USA> and the <Russia>
   """
   fig_text(
      0.127, 0.84,
      text,
      ha='left', va='top',
      fontsize=20,
      color='black',
      highlight_textprops=[
               {'color':colors[1],
               'fontweight':'bold'},
               {'color':colors[0],
               'fontweight':'bold'}
      ],
      fig=fig
   )

   # credit annotation
   text = """
   Developed by <@joseph_barbier>
   Data Source: <Office for Outer Space Affairs>
   """
   fig_text(
      0.9, 0,
      text,
      ha='right', va='bottom',
      fontsize=7,
      color='black',
      highlight_textprops=[
         {'fontweight':'bold'},
         {'fontweight':'bold'},
      ],
      fig=fig
   )

   # get text to display at current frame
   total_chars = len(description)
   effective_frame_count = len(pivot_df) - 20 # ends 20 frames before the end
   num_chars = math.ceil(total_chars * (frame / effective_frame_count)) if frame < effective_frame_count else total_chars
   current_description = description[:num_chars]
   wrapped_text = '\n'.join([textwrap.fill(paragraph, width=70) for paragraph in current_description.split('\n')])

   # display description
   fig_text(
      0.157, 0.72,
      wrapped_text,
      ha='left', va='top',
      fontsize=14,
      color='black',
      fig=fig
   )

   # display conclusion message on last frame
   last_frame = len(pivot_df) - 1
   if frame == last_frame:
      total_last = int(pivot_df.loc[total_objects_today].sum())
      total_usa = int(pivot_df.loc[total_objects_today, "USA"])
      total_rus = int(pivot_df.loc[total_objects_today, "RUS"])
      text = f"""
      Total Objects Launched in Outer Space in {total_objects_today}: <{total_last}>
      <USA>: <{total_usa}> ({total_usa/total_last*100:.2f}%)
      <Russia>: <{total_rus}> ({total_rus/total_last*100:.2f}%)
      """
      fig_text(
         0.43, 0.32,
         text,
         ha='center', va='center',
         fontsize=16,
         color='black',
         highlight_textprops=[
               {'fontweight': 'bold'},
               {'color': colors[1], 'fontweight': 'bold'},
               {'fontweight': 'bold'},
               {'color': colors[0], 'fontweight': 'bold'},
               {'fontweight': 'bold'},
         ],
         fig=fig
      )
   else:

      # annotate events
      for event_date, end_date, adj, event_text in zip(dates_highlight, event_end_dates, adjustements, event_texts):
         annot_event(year, event_date, end_date, adj, event_text)

   return ax

7f:T62d,# control resolution (lower is faster)
dpi = 30
plt.rcParams['figure.dpi'] = dpi
plt.rcParams['savefig.dpi'] = dpi

# parameters
colors = ['#2a9d8f', '#e76f51']
background_color = '#fef9ef'

# create figure and axis
fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(12, 8))
fig.set_facecolor(background_color)
ax.set_facecolor(background_color)

def update(frame):
    ax.clear()
    subset_df = pivot_df.iloc[:frame]
    ax.stackplot(
        subset_df.index,
        subset_df.values.T,
        colors=colors
    )

    # description of video
    text = """
    Evolution of the number of object put in outer
    space each year by the <USA> and the <Russia>
    """
    fig_text(
        0.127, 0.84,
        text,
        ha='left', va='top',
        fontsize=20,
        color='black',
        highlight_textprops=[
                {'color':colors[1],
                'fontweight':'bold'},
                {'color':colors[0],
                'fontweight':'bold'}
        ],
        fig=fig
    )

    # credit annotation
    text = """
    Developed by <@joseph_barbier>
    Data Source: <Office for Outer Space Affairs>
    """
    fig_text(
        0.9, 0,
        text,
        ha='right', va='bottom',
        fontsize=7,
        color='black',
        highlight_textprops=[
            {'fontweight':'bold'},
            {'fontweight':'bold'},
        ],
        fig=fig
    )

    return ax

ani = FuncAnimation(
    fig=fig,
    func=update,
    frames=len(pivot_df),
    interval=10 # in ms
)
ani.save('../../static/animations/web-animation-with-text2.gif', writer='imagemagick', fps=5)80:T2714,<pre class="shiki github-light" style="background-color:#fff;color:#24292e" tabindex="0"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#6A737D"># control resolution (lower is faster)</span></span>

81:T709,# control resolution (lower is faster)
dpi = 30
plt.rcParams['figure.dpi'] = dpi
plt.rcParams['savefig.dpi'] = dpi

# parameters
colors = ['#2a9d8f', '#e76f51']
background_color = '#fef9ef'

# create figure and axis
fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(12, 8))
fig.set_facecolor(background_color)
ax.set_facecolor(background_color)

def update(frame):
    ax.clear()
    subset_df = pivot_df.iloc[:frame]
    ax.stackplot(
        subset_df.index,
        subset_df.values.T,
        colors=colors
    )

    # custom axis
    factor_y_axis = 1.9
    y_max = pivot_df.iloc[:frame+1].sum(axis=1).max()*factor_y_axis
    ax.set_ylim(0, y_max)
    ax.set_xticks([])
    ax.spines[['top', 'right', 'bottom']].set_visible(False)

    # description of video
    text = """
    Evolution of the number of object put in outer
    space each year by the <USA> and the <Russia>
    """
    fig_text(
        0.127, 0.84,
        text,
        ha='left', va='top',
        fontsize=20,
        color='black',
        highlight_textprops=[
                {'color':colors[1],
                'fontweight':'bold'},
                {'color':colors[0],
                'fontweight':'bold'}
        ],
        fig=fig
    )

    # credit annotation
    text = """
    Developed by <@joseph_barbier>
    Data Source: <Office for Outer Space Affairs>
    """
    fig_text(
        0.9, 0,
        text,
        ha='right', va='bottom',
        fontsize=7,
        color='black',
        highlight_textprops=[
            {'fontweight':'bold'},
            {'fontweight':'bold'},
        ],
        fig=fig
    )

    return ax

ani = FuncAnimation(
    fig=fig,
    func=update,
    frames=len(pivot_df),
    interval=10 # in ms

54:["$","p","p-22",{"children":["In order to have a text that is displayed ",["$","strong","strong-0",{"children":"throughout the animation"}],", we need to find a way to define ",["$","strong","strong-1",{"children":"what part"}]," of the text we should display at each frame. Here is how it works:"]}]
55:["$","ol","ol-0",{"children":["\n",["$","li","li-0",{"children":["\n",["$","p","p-0",{"children":[["$","strong","strong-0",{"children":"Total Characters Calculation"}],": It first calculates the total number of characters in the full description using ",["$","code","code-0",{"children":"len(description)"}],", where ",["$","code","code-1",{"children":"description"}]," is the text we want to display."]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li","li-1",{"children":["\n",["$","p","p-0",{"children":[["$","strong","strong-0",{"children":"Effective Frame Count"}],": It determines the number of frames over which the text will appear, excluding the last 20 frames of the total number of frames in ",["$","code","code-0",{"children":"pivot_df"}],". Exclude the last 20 frames to ensure the text is displayed for a reasonable amount of time and ",["$","strong","strong-1",{"children":"ends before"}]," the animation ends."]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li","li-2",{"children":["\n",["$","p","p-0",{"children":[["$","strong","strong-0",{"children":"Characters per Frame"}],": It calculates how many characters of the description should be displayed up to the current frame. If the current frame is before the end of the effective frame count, it shows a portion of the text proportional to the current frame number. If the current frame number is at or beyond the effective frame count, it shows the entire text."]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li","li-3",{"children":["\n",["$","p","p-0",{"children":[["$","strong","strong-0",{"children":"Current Text"}],": The script then extracts the portion of the description up to the calculated number of characters."]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li","li-4",{"children":["\n",["$","p","p-0",{"children":[["$","strong","strong-0",{"children":"Text Wrapping"}],": Finally, it wraps the text to ensure each line does not exceed 70 characters, which helps in making the text easy to read on screen. Each paragraph is processed separately to maintain paragraph breaks."]}],"\n"]}],"\n"]}]
56:["$","p","p-23",{"children":["In order to make the code cleaner, we define ",["$","strong","strong-0",{"children":"on top of the script"}]," values that will be then used in annotations and in the text that appears throughout the animation."]}]
83:Tc9b,# control resolution (lower is faster)
dpi = 30
plt.rcParams['figure.dpi'] = dpi
plt.rcParams['savefig.dpi'] = dpi

# parameters
colors = ['#2a9d8f', '#e76f51']
background_color = '#fef9ef'

# reference values
total = pivot_df_reference.sum().sum()
us_share = pivot_df_reference.loc[:,'USA'].sum()/total*100
rus_share = 100 - us_share
data_after_2012 = pivot_df_reference.loc[pivot_df_reference.index >= 2012]
us_share_after_2012 = data_after_2012['USA'].sum()/data_after_2012.sum().sum()*100

# important dates
start_date = 1958
end_date = 2023

# description
description = f"""Between {start_date} and {end_date}, the USA and Russia have launched in outer space a total of {int(total)} objects. The USA has launched {us_share:.2f}% of these objects, while the Russia has only launched {rus_share:.2f}% of them.\n

After 2012, {us_share_after_2012:.2f}% of the objects launched in outer space by the USA and Russia were from the USA, highlighting the increasing dominance of the USA in outer space exploration.
"""

# create figure and axis
fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(12, 8))
fig.set_facecolor(background_color)
ax.set_facecolor(background_color)

def update(frame):
    ax.clear()
    subset_df = pivot_df.iloc[:frame]
    ax.stackplot(
        subset_df.index,
        subset_df.values.T,
        colors=colors
    )

    # custom axis
    factor_y_axis = 1.9
    y_max = pivot_df.iloc[:frame+1].sum(axis=1).max()*factor_y_axis
    ax.set_ylim(0, y_max)
    ax.set_xticks([])
    ax.spines[['top', 'right', 'bottom']].set_visible(False)

    # description of video
    text = """
    Evolution of the number of object put in outer
    space each year by the <USA> and the <Russia>
    """
    fig_text(
        0.127, 0.84,
        text,
        ha='left', va='top',
        fontsize=20,
        color='black',
        highlight_textprops=[
                {'color':colors[1],
                'fontweight':'bold'},
                {'color':colors[0],
                'fontweight':'bold'}
        ],
        fig=fig
    )

    # credit annotation
    text = """
    Developed by <@joseph_barbier>
    Data Source: <Office for Outer Space Affairs>
    """
    fig_text(
        0.9, 0,
        text,
        ha='right', va='bottom',
        fontsize=7,
        color='black',
        highlight_textprops=[
            {'fontweight':'bold'},
            {'fontweight':'bold'},
        ],
        fig=fig
    )

    # get text to display at current frame
    total_chars = len(description)
    effective_frame_count = len(pivot_df) - 20 # ends 20 frames before the end
    num_chars = math.ceil(total_chars * (frame / effective_frame_count)) if frame < effective_frame_count else total_chars
    current_description = description[:num_chars]
    wrapped_text = '\n'.join([textwrap.fill(paragraph, width=70) for paragraph in current_description.split('\n')])

    fig_text(
        0.157, 0.72,
        wrapped_text,
        ha='left', va='top',
        fontsize=14,
        color='black',
        fig=fig
    )

    return ax

ani = FuncAnimation(
    fig=fig,
    func=update,
    frames=len(pivot_df),
    interval=10 # in ms
)

5b:["$","ul","ul-0",{"children":["\n",["$","li","li-0",{"children":["\n",["$","p","p-0",{"children":["we create a ",["$","code","code-0",{"children":"annot_event()"}]," function that takes multiple arguments: ",["$","code","code-1",{"children":"current_date"}],", ",["$","code","code-2",{"children":"event_start_date"}],", ",["$","code","code-3",{"children":"event_end_date"}],", ",["$","code","code-4",{"children":"adj"}]," and ",["$","code","code-5",{"children":"text"}],"."]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li","li-1",{"children":["\n",["$","p","p-0",{"children":["with this information, we can find ",["$","strong","strong-0",{"children":"which event"}]," should be currently highlighted and display the corresponding text. 5d:["$","p","p-27",{"children":["$","code","code-0",{"children":"# year annotation in background"}]}]
85:T1622,# control resolution (lower is faster)
dpi = 30
plt.rcParams['figure.dpi'] = dpi
plt.rcParams['savefig.dpi'] = dpi

# parameters
colors = ['#2a9d8f', '#e76f51']
background_color = '#fef9ef'

# reference values
total = pivot_df_reference.sum().sum()
us_share = pivot_df_reference.loc[:,'USA'].sum()/total*100
rus_share = 100 - us_share
data_after_2012 = pivot_df_reference.loc[pivot_df_reference.index >= 2012]
us_share_after_2012 = data_after_2012['USA'].sum()/data_after_2012.sum().sum()*100

# important dates
start_date = 1958
end_date = 2023
first_human_spaceflight = 1961
us_moon_landing = 1969
hubble_launch = 1990
internat_space_station = 1998
earth_size_found = 2017
photo_black_hole = 2019
total_objects_today = 2023

# delay for each event
delay_years = 1 # to display the event one year after it happened
dates_highlight = [
    first_human_spaceflight + delay_years,
    us_moon_landing + delay_years,
    hubble_launch + delay_years,
    internat_space_station + delay_years,
    earth_size_found + delay_years,
    photo_black_hole + delay_years,
]
event_end_dates = dates_highlight[1:] + [end_date + delay_years]

# position adjustments for each event
adjustements = [
    (0, 0),
    (0, 10),
    (2, 5),
    (0, 20),
    (-18, 0),
    (-22, -400),
]

# text to display for each event
event_texts = [
    f'First Human Spaceflight\n<{first_human_spaceflight}>',
    f'US Moon Landing\n<{us_moon_landing}>',
    f'Hubble Launch\n<{hubble_launch}>',
    f'International Space Station\n<{internat_space_station}>',
    f'Earth Size Planets found\n<{earth_size_found}>',
    f'First Image of a Black Hole\n<{photo_black_hole}>'
]

# description
description = f"""Between {start_date} and {end_date}, the USA and Russia have launched in outer space a total of {int(total)} objects. The USA has launched {us_share:.2f}% of these objects, while the Russia has only launched {rus_share:.2f}% of them.\n

After 2012, {us_share_after_2012:.2f}% of the objects launched in outer space by the USA and Russia were from the USA, highlighting the increasing dominance of the USA in outer space exploration.
"""

# create figure and axis
fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(12, 8))
fig.set_facecolor(background_color)
ax.set_facecolor(background_color)

# functions use to annotate events with black lollipops
def annot_event(current_date, event_start_date, event_end_date, adj, text):

   # condition to only display one event at a time
   if event_start_date <= current_date <= event_end_date:

      # filter on the current year
      sub_subset = pivot_df.loc[event_start_date]
      total_objects_thisyear = sub_subset['RUS'] + sub_subset['USA']

      # plot lollipop
      ax.plot((event_start_date, event_start_date), (0, total_objects_thisyear), color='black', zorder=10)
      ax.scatter(event_start_date, total_objects_thisyear, color='black', s=100, zorder=10)

      # annotate event
      ax_text(
         event_start_date - 2 + adj[0], # x position
         total_objects_thisyear*1.2 + adj[1], # y position
         text,
         fontsize=14,
         color='black',
         ha='left', va='center',
         highlight_textprops=[
               {'fontweight':'bold'}
         ],
         ax=ax
      )

def update(frame):
   ax.clear()
   subset_df = pivot_df.iloc[:frame]
   ax.stackplot(
      subset_df.index,
      subset_df.values.T,
      colors=colors
   )

   # custom axis
   factor_y_axis = 1.9
   y_max = pivot_df.iloc[:frame+1].sum(axis=1).max()*factor_y_axis
   ax.set_ylim(0, y_max)
   ax.set_xticks([])
   ax.spines[['top', 'right', 'bottom']].set_visible(False)

   # year annotation in background
   year = pivot_df.index[frame]
   fig_text(
      0.15, 0.9,
      '1958 - ' + str(round(year)),
      ha='left', va='center',
      fontsize=50,
      fontweight='bold',
      alpha=0.1,
      fig=fig
   )

   # description of video
   text = """
   Evolution of the number of object put in outer
   space each year by the <USA> and the <Russia>
   """
   fig_text(
      0.127, 0.84,
      text,
      ha='left', va='top',
      fontsize=20,
      color='black',
      highlight_textprops=[
               {'color':colors[1],
               'fontweight':'bold'},
               {'color':colors[0],
               'fontweight':'bold'}
      ],
      fig=fig
   )

   # credit annotation
   text = """
   Developed by <@joseph_barbier>
   Data Source: <Office for Outer Space Affairs>
   """
   fig_text(
      0.9, 0,
      text,
      ha='right', va='bottom',
      fontsize=7,
      color='black',
      highlight_textprops=[
         {'fontweight':'bold'},
         {'fontweight':'bold'},
      ],
      fig=fig
   )

   # get text to display at current frame
   total_chars = len(description)
   effective_frame_count = len(pivot_df) - 20 # ends 20 frames before the end
   num_chars = math.ceil(total_chars * (frame / effective_frame_count)) if frame < effective_frame_count else total_chars
   current_description = description[:num_chars]
   wrapped_text = '\n'.join([textwrap.fill(paragraph, width=70) for paragraph in current_description.split('\n')])

   # display description
   fig_text(
      0.157, 0.72,
      wrapped_text,
      ha='left', va='top',
      fontsize=14,
      color='black',
      fig=fig
   )

   # annotate events
   for event_date, end_date, adj, event_text in zip(dates_highlight, event_end_dates, adjustements, event_texts):
      annot_event(year, event_date, end_date, adj, event_text)

   return ax

ani = FuncAnimation(
    fig=fig,
    func=update,
    frames=len(pivot_df),
    interval=10 # in ms
)
ani.save('../../static/animations/web-animation-with-text5.gif', writer='imagemagick', fps=5)90:I[27201,["$2","$3","$4","$5"],"IconMark"]

<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">plt.rcParams[</span><span style="color:#032F62">'savefig.dpi'</span><span style="color:#24292E">] </span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#24292E"> dpi</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#6A737D"># reference values</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">total </span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#24292E"> pivot_df_reference.sum().sum()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">us_share </span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#24292E"> pivot_df_reference.loc[:,</span><span style="color:#032F62">'USA'</span><span style="color:#24292E">].sum()</span><span style="color:#D73A49">/</span><span style="color:#24292E">total</span><span style="color:#D73A49">*</span><span style="color:#005CC5">100</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">rus_share </span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#005CC5"> 100</span><span style="color:#D73A49"> -</span><span style="color:#24292E"> us_share</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">data_after_2012 </span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#24292E"> pivot_df_reference.loc[pivot_df_reference.index </span><span style="color:#D73A49">>=</span><span style="color:#005CC5"> 2012</span><span style="color:#24292E">]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">us_share_after_2012 </span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#24292E"> data_after_2012[</span><span style="color:#032F62">'USA'</span><span style="color:#24292E">].sum()</span><span style="color:#D73A49">/</span><span style="color:#24292E">data_after_2012.sum().sum()</span><span style="color:#D73A49">*</span><span style="color:#005CC5">100</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6A737D"># important dates</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">start_date </span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#005CC5"> 1958</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">end_date </span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#005CC5"> 2023</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">first_human_spaceflight </span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#005CC5"> 1961</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">us_moon_landing </span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#005CC5"> 1969</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">hubble_launch </span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#005CC5"> 1990</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">internat_space_station </span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#005CC5"> 1998</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">earth_size_found </span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#005CC5"> 2017</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">photo_black_hole </span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#005CC5"> 2019</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">total_objects_today </span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#005CC5"> 2023</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6A737D"># delay for each event</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">delay_years </span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#005CC5"> 1</span><span style="color:#6A737D"> # to display the event one year after it happened</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">dates_highlight </span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#24292E"> [</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">    first_human_spaceflight </span><span style="color:#D73A49">+</span><span style="color:#24292E"> delay_years,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">    us_moon_landing </span><span style="color:#D73A49">+</span><span style="color:#24292E"> delay_years,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">    hubble_launch </span><span style="color:#D73A49">+</span><span style="color:#24292E"> delay_years,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">    internat_space_station </span><span style="color:#D73A49">+</span><span style="color:#24292E"> delay_years,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">    earth_size_found </span><span style="color:#D73A49">+</span><span style="color:#24292E"> delay_years,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">    photo_black_hole </span><span style="color:#D73A49">+</span><span style="color:#24292E"> delay_years,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">event_end_dates </span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#24292E"> dates_highlight[</span><span style="color:#005CC5">1</span><span style="color:#24292E">:] </span><span style="color:#D73A49">+</span><span style="color:#24292E"> [end_date </span><span style="color:#D73A49">+</span><span style="color:#24292E"> delay_years]</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6A737D"># position adjustments for each event</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">adjustements </span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#24292E"> [</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">    (</span><span style="color:#005CC5">0</span><span style="color:#24292E">, </span><span style="color:#005CC5">0</span><span style="color:#24292E">),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">    (</span><span style="color:#005CC5">0</span><span style="color:#24292E">, </span><span style="color:#005CC5">10</span><span style="color:#24292E">),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">    (</span><span style="color:#005CC5">2</span><span style="color:#24292E">, </span><span style="color:#005CC5">5</span><span style="color:#24292E">),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">    (</span><span style="color:#005CC5">0</span><span style="color:#24292E">, </span><span style="color:#005CC5">20</span><span style="color:#24292E">),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">    (</span><span style="color:#D73A49">-</span><span style="color:#005CC5">18</span><span style="color:#24292E">, </span><span style="color:#005CC5">0</span><span style="color:#24292E">),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">    (</span><span style="color:#D73A49">-</span><span style="color:#005CC5">22</span><span style="color:#24292E">, </span><span style="color:#D73A49">-</span><span style="color:#005CC5">400</span><span style="color:#24292E">),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">]</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6A737D"># text to display for each event</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">event_texts </span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#24292E"> [</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49">    f</span><span style="color:#032F62">'First Human Spaceflight</span><span style="color:#005CC5">\n</span><span style="color:#032F62">&#x3C;</span><span style="color:#005CC5">{</span><span style="color:#24292E">first_human_spaceflight</span><span style="color:#005CC5">}</span><span style="color:#032F62">>'</span><span style="color:#24292E">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49">    f</span><span style="color:#032F62">'US Moon Landing</span><span style="color:#005CC5">\n</span><span style="color:#032F62">&#x3C;</span><span style="color:#005CC5">{</span><span style="color:#24292E">us_moon_landing</span><span style="color:#005CC5">}</span><span style="color:#032F62">>'</span><span style="color:#24292E">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49">    f</span><span style="color:#032F62">'Hubble Launch</span><span style="color:#005CC5">\n</span><span style="color:#032F62">&#x3C;</span><span style="color:#005CC5">{</span><span style="color:#24292E">hubble_launch</span><span style="color:#005CC5">}</span><span style="color:#032F62">>'</span><span style="color:#24292E">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49">    f</span><span style="color:#032F62">'International Space Station</span><span style="color:#005CC5">\n</span><span style="color:#032F62">&#x3C;</span><span style="color:#005CC5">{</span><span style="color:#24292E">internat_space_station</span><span style="color:#005CC5">}</span><span style="color:#032F62">>'</span><span style="color:#24292E">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49">    f</span><span style="color:#032F62">'Earth Size Planets found</span><span style="color:#005CC5">\n</span><span style="color:#032F62">&#x3C;</span><span style="color:#005CC5">{</span><span style="color:#24292E">earth_size_found</span><span style="color:#005CC5">}</span><span style="color:#032F62">>'</span><span style="color:#24292E">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49">    f</span><span style="color:#032F62">'First Image of a Black Hole</span><span style="color:#005CC5">\n</span><span style="color:#032F62">&#x3C;</span><span style="color:#005CC5">{</span><span style="color:#24292E">photo_black_hole</span><span style="color:#005CC5">}</span><span style="color:#032F62">>'</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">]</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6A737D"># description</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">description </span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#D73A49"> f</span><span style="color:#032F62">"""Between </span><span style="color:#005CC5">{</span><span style="color:#24292E">start_date</span><span style="color:#005CC5">}</span><span style="color:#032F62"> and </span><span style="color:#005CC5">{</span><span style="color:#24292E">end_date</span><span style="color:#005CC5">}</span><span style="color:#032F62">, the USA and Russia have launched in outer space a total of </span><span style="color:#005CC5">{int</span><span style="color:#24292E">(total)</span><span style="color:#005CC5">}</span><span style="color:#032F62"> objects. The USA has launched </span><span style="color:#005CC5">{</span><span style="color:#24292E">us_share</span><span style="color:#D73A49">:.2f</span><span style="color:#005CC5">}</span><span style="color:#032F62">% of these objects, while the Russia has only launched </span><span style="color:#005CC5">{</span><span style="color:#24292E">rus_share</span><span style="color:#D73A49">:.2f</span><span style="color:#005CC5">}</span><span style="color:#032F62">% of them.</span><span style="color:#005CC5">\n</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#032F62">After 2012, </span><span style="color:#005CC5">{</span><span style="color:#24292E">us_share_after_2012</span><span style="color:#D73A49">:.2f</span><span style="color:#005CC5">}</span><span style="color:#032F62">% of the objects launched in outer space by the USA and Russia were from the USA, highlighting the increasing dominance of the USA in outer space exploration.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#032F62">"""</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6A737D"># create figure and axis</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">fig, ax </span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#24292E"> plt.subplots(</span><span style="color:#E36209">figsize</span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#24292E">(</span><span style="color:#005CC5">12</span><span style="color:#24292E">, </span><span style="color:#005CC5">8</span><span style="color:#24292E">))</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">fig.set_facecolor(background_color)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">ax.set_facecolor(background_color)</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6A737D"># functions use to annotate events with black lollipops</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49">def</span><span style="color:#6F42C1"> annot_event</span><span style="color:#24292E">(current_date, event_start_date, event_end_date, adj, text):</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6A737D">   # condition to only display one event at a time</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49">   if</span><span style="color:#24292E"> event_start_date </span><span style="color:#D73A49">&#x3C;=</span><span style="color:#24292E"> current_date </span><span style="color:#D73A49">&#x3C;=</span><span style="color:#24292E"> event_end_date:</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6A737D">      # filter on the current year</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">      sub_subset </span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#24292E"> pivot_df.loc[event_start_date]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">      total_objects_thisyear </span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#24292E"> sub_subset[</span><span style="color:#032F62">'RUS'</span><span style="color:#24292E">] </span><span style="color:#D73A49">+</span><span style="color:#24292E"> sub_subset[</span><span style="color:#032F62">'USA'</span><span style="color:#24292E">]</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6A737D">      # plot lollipop</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">      ax.plot((event_start_date, event_start_date), (</span><span style="color:#005CC5">0</span><span style="color:#24292E">, total_objects_thisyear), </span><span style="color:#E36209">color</span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#032F62">'black'</span><span style="color:#24292E">, </span><span style="color:#E36209">zorder</span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#005CC5">10</span><span style="color:#24292E">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">      ax.scatter(event_start_date, total_objects_thisyear, </span><span style="color:#E36209">color</span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#032F62">'black'</span><span style="color:#24292E">, </span><span style="color:#E36209">s</span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#005CC5">100</span><span style="color:#24292E">, </span><span style="color:#E36209">zorder</span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#005CC5">10</span><span style="color:#24292E">)</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6A737D">      # annotate event</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">      ax_text(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">         event_start_date </span><span style="color:#D73A49">-</span><span style="color:#005CC5"> 2</span><span style="color:#D73A49"> +</span><span style="color:#24292E"> adj[</span><span style="color:#005CC5">0</span><span style="color:#24292E">], </span><span style="color:#6A737D"># x position</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">         total_objects_thisyear</span><span style="color:#D73A49">*</span><span style="color:#005CC5">1.2</span><span style="color:#D73A49"> +</span><span style="color:#24292E"> adj[</span><span style="color:#005CC5">1</span><span style="color:#24292E">], </span><span style="color:#6A737D"># y position</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">         text,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E36209">         fontsize</span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#005CC5">14</span><span style="color:#24292E">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E36209">         color</span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#032F62">'black'</span><span style="color:#24292E">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E36209">         ha</span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#032F62">'left'</span><span style="color:#24292E">, </span><span style="color:#E36209">va</span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#032F62">'center'</span><span style="color:#24292E">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E36209">         highlight_textprops</span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#24292E">[</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">               {</span><span style="color:#032F62">'fontweight'</span><span style="color:#24292E">:</span><span style="color:#032F62">'bold'</span><span style="color:#24292E">}</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">         ],</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E36209">         ax</span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#24292E">ax</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">      )</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49">def</span><span style="color:#6F42C1"> update</span><span style="color:#24292E">(frame):</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">   ax.clear()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">   subset_df </span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#24292E"> pivot_df.iloc[:frame]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">   ax.stackplot(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">      subset_df.index,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">      subset_df.values.T,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E36209">      colors</span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#24292E">colors</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">   )</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6A737D">   # custom axis</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">   factor_y_axis </span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#005CC5"> 1.9</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">   y_max </span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#24292E"> pivot_df.iloc[:frame</span><span style="color:#D73A49">+</span><span style="color:#005CC5">1</span><span style="color:#24292E">].sum(</span><span style="color:#E36209">axis</span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#005CC5">1</span><span style="color:#24292E">).max()</span><span style="color:#D73A49">*</span><span style="color:#24292E">factor_y_axis</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">   ax.set_ylim(</span><span style="color:#005CC5">0</span><span style="color:#24292E">, y_max)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">   ax.set_xticks([])</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">   ax.spines[[</span><span style="color:#032F62">'top'</span><span style="color:#24292E">, </span><span style="color:#032F62">'right'</span><span style="color:#24292E">, </span><span style="color:#032F62">'bottom'</span><span style="color:#24292E">]].set_visible(</span><span style="color:#005CC5">False</span><span style="color:#24292E">)</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6A737D">   # year annotation in background</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">   year </span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#24292E"> pivot_df.index[frame]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">   fig_text(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#005CC5">      0.15</span><span style="color:#24292E">, </span><span style="color:#005CC5">0.9</span><span style="color:#24292E">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#032F62">      '1958 - '</span><span style="color:#D73A49"> +</span><span style="color:#005CC5"> str</span><span style="color:#24292E">(</span><span style="color:#005CC5">round</span><span style="color:#24292E">(year)),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E36209">      ha</span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#032F62">'left'</span><span style="color:#24292E">, </span><span style="color:#E36209">va</span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#032F62">'center'</span><span style="color:#24292E">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E36209">      fontsize</span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#005CC5">50</span><span style="color:#24292E">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E36209">      fontweight</span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#032F62">'bold'</span><span style="color:#24292E">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E36209">      alpha</span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#005CC5">0.1</span><span style="color:#24292E">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E36209">      fig</span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#24292E">fig</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">   )</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6A737D">   # description of video</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">   text </span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#032F62"> """</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#032F62">   Evolution of the number of object put in outer</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#032F62">   space each year by the &#x3C;USA> and the &#x3C;Russia></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#032F62">   """</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">   fig_text(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#005CC5">      0.127</span><span style="color:#24292E">, </span><span style="color:#005CC5">0.84</span><span style="color:#24292E">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">      text,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E36209">      ha</span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#032F62">'left'</span><span style="color:#24292E">, </span><span style="color:#E36209">va</span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#032F62">'top'</span><span style="color:#24292E">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E36209">      fontsize</span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#005CC5">20</span><span style="color:#24292E">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E36209">      color</span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#032F62">'black'</span><span style="color:#24292E">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E36209">      highlight_textprops</span><span style="color:#D73A49">=</span><span style="color:#24292E">[</span></span>

87:T1cba,# control resolution (lower is faster)
dpi = 30
plt.rcParams['figure.dpi'] = dpi
plt.rcParams['savefig.dpi'] = dpi

# parameters
colors = ['#2a9d8f', '#e76f51']
background_color = '#fef9ef'

# reference values
total = pivot_df_reference.sum().sum()
us_share = pivot_df_reference.loc[:,'USA'].sum()/total*100
rus_share = 100 - us_share
data_after_2012 = pivot_df_reference.loc[pivot_df_reference.index >= 2012]
us_share_after_2012 = data_after_2012['USA'].sum()/data_after_2012.sum().sum()*100

# important dates
start_date = 1958
end_date = 2023
first_human_spaceflight = 1961
us_moon_landing = 1969
hubble_launch = 1990
internat_space_station = 1998
earth_size_found = 2017
photo_black_hole = 2019
total_objects_today = 2023

# delay for each event
delay_years = 1 # to display the event one year after it happened
dates_highlight = [
    first_human_spaceflight + delay_years,
    us_moon_landing + delay_years,
    hubble_launch + delay_years,
    internat_space_station + delay_years,
    earth_size_found + delay_years,
    photo_black_hole + delay_years,
]
event_end_dates = dates_highlight[1:] + [end_date + delay_years]

# position adjustments for each event
adjustements = [
    (0, 0),
    (0, 10),
    (2, 5),
    (0, 20),
    (-18, 0),
    (-22, -400),
]

# text to display for each event
event_texts = [
    f'First Human Spaceflight\n<{first_human_spaceflight}>',
    f'US Moon Landing\n<{us_moon_landing}>',
    f'Hubble Launch\n<{hubble_launch}>',
    f'International Space Station\n<{internat_space_station}>',
    f'Earth Size Planets found\n<{earth_size_found}>',
    f'First Image of a Black Hole\n<{photo_black_hole}>'
]

# description
description = f"""Between {start_date} and {end_date}, the USA and Russia have launched in outer space a total of {int(total)} objects. The USA has launched {us_share:.2f}% of these objects, while the Russia has only launched {rus_share:.2f}% of them.\n

After 2012, {us_share_after_2012:.2f}% of the objects launched in outer space by the USA and Russia were from the USA, highlighting the increasing dominance of the USA in outer space exploration.
"""

# create figure and axis
fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(12, 8))
fig.set_facecolor(background_color)
ax.set_facecolor(background_color)

# functions use to annotate events with black lollipops
def annot_event(current_date, event_start_date, event_end_date, adj, text):

   # condition to only display one event at a time
   if event_start_date <= current_date <= event_end_date:

      # filter on the current year
      sub_subset = pivot_df.loc[event_start_date]
      total_objects_thisyear = sub_subset['RUS'] + sub_subset['USA']

      # plot lollipop
      ax.plot((event_start_date, event_start_date), (0, total_objects_thisyear), color='black', zorder=10)
      ax.scatter(event_start_date, total_objects_thisyear, color='black', s=100, zorder=10)

      # annotate event
      ax_text(
         event_start_date - 2 + adj[0], # x position
         total_objects_thisyear*1.2 + adj[1], # y position
         text,
         fontsize=14,
         color='black',
         ha='left', va='center',
         highlight_textprops=[
               {'fontweight':'bold'}
         ],
         ax=ax
      )

def update(frame):
   ax.clear()
   subset_df = pivot_df.iloc[:frame]
   ax.stackplot(
      subset_df.index,
      subset_df.values.T,
      colors=colors
   )

   # custom axis
   factor_y_axis = 1.9
   y_max = pivot_df.iloc[:frame+1].sum(axis=1).max()*factor_y_axis
   ax.set_ylim(0, y_max)
   ax.set_xticks([])
   ax.spines[['top', 'right', 'bottom']].set_visible(False)

   # year annotation in background
   year = pivot_df.index[frame]
   fig_text(
      0.15, 0.9,
      '1958 - ' + str(round(year)),
      ha='left', va='center',
      fontsize=50,
      fontweight='bold',
      alpha=0.1,
      fig=fig
   )

   # description of video
   text = """
   Evolution of the number of object put in outer
   space each year by the <USA> and the <Russia>
   """
   fig_text(
      0.127, 0.84,
      text,
      ha='left', va='top',
      fontsize=20,
      color='black',
      highlight_textprops=[
               {'color':colors[1],
               'fontweight':'bold'},
               {'color':colors[0],
               'fontweight':'bold'}
      ],
      fig=fig
   )

   # credit annotation
   text = """
   Developed by <@joseph_barbier>
   Data Source: <Office for Outer Space Affairs>
   """
   fig_text(
      0.9, 0,
      text,
      ha='right', va='bottom',
      fontsize=7,
      color='black',
      highlight_textprops=[
         {'fontweight':'bold'},
         {'fontweight':'bold'},
      ],
      fig=fig
   )

   # get text to display at current frame
   total_chars = len(description)
   effective_frame_count = len(pivot_df) - 20 # ends 20 frames before the end
   num_chars = math.ceil(total_chars * (frame / effective_frame_count)) if frame < effective_frame_count else total_chars
   current_description = description[:num_chars]
   wrapped_text = '\n'.join([textwrap.fill(paragraph, width=70) for paragraph in current_description.split('\n')])

   # display description
   fig_text(
      0.157, 0.72,
      wrapped_text,
      ha='left', va='top',
      fontsize=14,
      color='black',
      fig=fig
   )

   # display conclusion message on last frame
   last_frame = len(pivot_df) - 1
   if frame == last_frame:
      total_last = int(pivot_df.loc[total_objects_today].sum())
      total_usa = int(pivot_df.loc[total_objects_today, "USA"])
      total_rus = int(pivot_df.loc[total_objects_today, "RUS"])
      text = f"""
      Total Objects Launched in Outer Space in {total_objects_today}: <{total_last}>
      <USA>: <{total_usa}> ({total_usa/total_last*100:.2f}%)
      <Russia>: <{total_rus}> ({total_rus/total_last*100:.2f}%)
      """
      fig_text(
         0.43, 0.32,
         text,
         ha='center', va='center',
         fontsize=16,
         color='black',
         highlight_textprops=[
               {'fontweight': 'bold'},
               {'color': colors[1], 'fontweight': 'bold'},
               {'fontweight': 'bold'},
               {'color': colors[0], 'fontweight': 'bold'},
               {'fontweight': 'bold'},
         ],
         fig=fig
      )
   else:

      # annotate events
      for event_date, end_date, adj, event_text in zip(dates_highlight, event_end_dates, adjustements, event_texts):
         annot_event(year, event_date, end_date, adj, event_text)

   return ax

ani = FuncAnimation(
    fig=fig,
    func=update,
    frames=len(pivot_df),
    interval=10 # in ms
)
path = '../../static/animations/web-animation-with-text6.gif'
ani.save(path, writer='imagemagick', fps=5)

# get initial frames
with Image.open(path) as img:
    frames = [frame.copy() for frame in ImageSequence.Iterator(img)]

# set duration of each frame
initial_duration = 50 # in ms
for i, frame in enumerate(frames):

   # default duration
   frame_duration = initial_duration

   # make last N frames becomes step by step slower
   N = 12
   if i > len(frames) - N:
      frame_duration = initial_duration + (i - len(frames) + N) * 50

   # stay for last date
   if i == len(frames) - 1:
      frame_duration = 10000

   # assign duration to the frame
   frame.info['duration'] = frame_duration

# save the frames
frames[0].save(path, save_all=True, append_images=frames[1:], loop=0)88:Taeb1,<pre class="shiki github-light" style="background-color:#fff;color:#24292e" tabindex="0"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#6A737D"># control resolution (lower is faster)</span></span>
